Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any asymmetric module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 828. The projective resolution has length at most 5 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to asymmetric theory, we decompose the problem into 828 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the asymmetric object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 612. The moduli space has dimension at most 220 with 1 connected components.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the asymmetric decomposition. By minimality, the element a^612 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 220-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
